		And rove the bravest riders Scythia nurtures,         	520
They whom thou ne’er could’st lure to walls of stone, 
	Nor rouse to war, save for their own free soil.
	These gained, defy the foe;
		Let him pursue and space itself engulphs him.	524
Omartes answered - ‘With the towers I built 
	Must I, O Kind adviser, stand or fall.
	Kings are not merely men -
		Epochs their lives, their actions the world’s story.	528
I sought to wean my people from the wild, 
	To centre scattered valours, wasted thoughts,
	Into one mind, a State;
		Failing in this, my life as king has perished:	532
And as mere man I should disdain to live. 
	Deemest thou now I could go back content
	A Scytb among the scyths ?
		I am no eaglet - I have borne the aegis. 	536
‘But life, as life, suffices youth for joy.
	Young plants win sunbeams, shift them as we may.
	So to the Nomad tribes
		Lead thou their Queen. - O sa”ve, ye gods, my daughter!’ 	540
The king’s proud head bowed o’er the hierarch’s breast. 
	‘Not unto me confide that precious charge,’
	Replied the sweet voiced seer ;
		Thou hast a choice of flight, I none. Thou choosest 	544 
‘To stand or fall, as stand or fall thy towers; 
	Priests may not choose; they stand or fall by shrines.
	Thus stand we both, or fall,
		Thou by the throne, and I beside the altar. 	548
But to thy child, ev’n in this funeral hour, 
	Give the sole lawful guardian failing thee;
	Let her free will elect
		From thy brave warriors him her heart most leans to;   	552
‘And pass with him along the secret way. 
	To lengthen yet the line of Scythian Kings,
	Meanwhile, since needs must be
		We trust to others this long-guarded secret.	 556
Choose one to whom I may impart the clue 
	Of the dark labyrinth; for a guide it needs;
	Be he in war well tried,
		And of high mark among the Nomad riders; 	560
Such as may say unto the antique tribes 
	With voice of one reared up among themselves,
	From walls of stone I bring
		Your King’s child to your tents; let Scythia guard her	564
‘Well do thy counsels please me,’ said the King.
	‘I will convene to such penurious feast ,
	As stint permits, the chiefs
		Worthiest to be the sires of warlike monarchs:        	568
‘And, following ancient custom with the Scyths, 
	He unto whom my daughther, with free choice,
	The wine - cup brimming gives,
		Shall take my blessing and go hence her husband.   	572
‘But since, for guide and leader of the few 
	That for such service are most keen and apt,
	The man in war most tried,
		And with the Nomads most esteemed, is Seuthes,   	576
Him to thy skilled instructions and full trust 
	Will I send straight.   Meanwhile go seek my child,
	And, as to her all thought
		Of her own safety in mine hour of peril 	580
Will in itself be hateful, use the force 
	That dwells on sacred lips with blandest art;
	Say that her presence here
		Palsies mine arm and dulls my brain with terror;    	584
That mine own safety I consult in hers,
	And let her hopeful think, that, tho’ we part, 
	The same road opes for both;
		And if walls fail me, hers will be my refuge.	 588
Omartes spoke, and of his stalwart chiefs 
	‘Selecting all the bravest yet unwived,
	He bade them to his board
		The following night, on matters of grave import;     	592
To Seuthes then the secret he disclosed,
	And Seuthes sought the hiefarch, conned the elite.
	And third the darksome maze
		To either issue, sepulchre and palace; 	596
And thus instructed, treasu’re, town, and king 
	Thus in his hands for bargain with the foe
	The treason schemed of yore, 
		Foiled when the Mede rejected Scythian nuptials,   	600
Yet oft revolved - as some pale hope deferred, 
	Seen indistinct in rearward depths of time 
	Flashed as, when looked for least,
		Thro’ the rent cloud of battle flashes triumph.        	604
And. reasoning with himself, ‘the Mede’, he said 
	‘Recks not who sits upon the Scythian throne,
	So that the ruler pay
		Grains of waste soil and drops of useless water:      	608
And if I render up an easy prey
	The senseless king refusing terms so mild,
	For such great service done
		And for my rank among the Scythian riders, 	612
‘The Mede would deem no man so fit as I
	To fill the throne; whose heir he scorned as wife,
	And yield him dust and drops,
		Holding the fealmS.and treasures of Omartes.’ 	616
So, when the next day’s sun began to slope,
	The traitor stood before Zariades, 
	Gaining the hostile camp
		From the mute grave.mound of his Scythian fathers. 	620
Plain as his simplest soldier’s was the tent 
	Wherein the lord of half the Orient sate,
	Alone in anxious thought,
		Intent on new device to quicken conquest. 	624
But for the single sapphire in his helm, 
	And near his hand the regal silver urn,
	Filled with the sparkling lymph,
		Which, whatsoe’er the distance, pure Choaspes      	628
Sends to the lips of Achaemenian kings, 
	The Asian ruler might to Spartan eyes
	Have seemed the hardy type	
		Of Europe’s manhood crowned in Lacedaemon.      	632
The traitor, sure of welcome, told his tale, 
	Proffered the treason and implied the terms.
	Then spoke Zariades;
		‘Know that all kings regard as foe in common       	636
‘The man who is a traitor to his king. 
	’Tis true that I thy treason must accept.
	I owe it to my hosts
		To scorn no means destroying their destroyer,        	640
‘But I will place no traitor on a throne. 
	Yet, since thy treason saves me many lives,
	I for their sake spare thine:
		And since thy deed degrades thee from the freeman, 	644
‘I add to life what slaves most covet - gold 
	They service done, seek lands where gold is king;
	And, tho’ thyself a slave,
		Buy freemen vile eno’ to call thee master.	648
But if thy promise fail, thy word ensnare, 
	Thy guidance blunder, by they side stalks death. 
	Death does not scare the man 
		Who, like thyself, has looked on it in battle;	652
‘But death in battle has a warrior’s gravel;
	A traitor dead - the vultures and the dogs.
	Then to close guard the King 
		Consigned the Scyth, who for the first time trembled;  	 656
And called in haste, and armed his Sacred Band,
	The Persian flower of all his Orient hosts;
	And soon in that .dark pass
		Marched war, led under rampired walls by treason.	660
Safe thro’, the fatal maze the Persians reached 
	Stairs winding upward into palace halls,
	With stealthy hand the guide,
		Pressed on the spring of the concealed portal,         	664
And slowly opening, peered within: the space
	Stood void; for so it had been planned, that none
	Might, when the hour arrived
		Obstruct the spot at which escape should vanish:    	668
But farther on, voices were heard confused, 
	And lights shone faintly thro’ the chinks of doors, 
	Where one less spacious hall 
		Led, also void, to that of fated banquet.	672
Curious, and yielding to his own bold heart, 
	As line on line came, steel-clad, from the wall,
	Flooding funereal floors, 
		The young King whispered, ‘Here await my signal,’ 	676
And stole along the intervening space, 
	At whose far end, curtains of Lydian woof,
	Between vast columns drawn,
		Left a slight crevice where their folds disparted;      	680
He looked within, unseen; all eyes were turned 
	Towards a pale front, just- risen o’er the guests,
	In which the Persian knew
		His brother King; it was not pale in battle. 	684
And thus Omartes spoke: - ‘Captains and sons 
	Of the same mother, Scythia, to this feast,
	Which in such straits of want
		Needs strong excuse, not idly are ye summoned.     	688
Wishing the line of kings from which I spring 
	Yet to extend, perchance, to happier times,
	And save mine only child
		From death, or, worse than death, the Median bondage,  	692
‘I would this night betroth her as a bride
	To him amongst you whom herself shall choose;
	And the benignant gods
		Have, thro’ the wisdom of their sacred augur,        	696
‘Shown me the means which may elude the foe, 
	And lead the two that in themselves unite
	The valour and the sway
		Of Scythia where her plains defy besiegers. 	700
If the gods bless the escape they thus permit,
	Braved first, as fitting, by a child of kings,
	Then the same means will free
		Flight for all those who give to siege its terror;       	704
Women and infants, wounded men and old, 
	If few by few, yet night by night, sent forth,
	Will leave no pang in death
		To those reserved to join the souls of heroes.        	708
As, in the hush of eve, a sudden wind
	Thrills thro’ a grove and bows the crest of pines,
	So crept a murmured hum
		Thro’ the grave banquet, and plumed heads bent downward: 	712
Till hushed each whisper, and upraised each eye, 
	As from a door behind the royal dais
	Into the conclave came
		The priest Teleutias leading the King’s daughter.     	716
‘Lift up thy veil, my child, Argiope,’ 
	Omartes said.   ‘And look around the board,
	And from yon beakers fill
		The cup I kiss as in thy hand I place it.	720
‘And whosoever from that hand receives 
	The cup, shaH. be thy husband and my son.’
	The virgin raised her veil;
		Shone on the hall the starlight of her beauty. 	724
But to no face amid the breathless guests
	Turned downcast lids from which the tears dropped slow:
	Passive she took the cup,
		With passive step led by the whispering augur 	728
Where, blazing lustre back upon the lamps, 
	Stood golden beakers under purple pall. 
	‘Courage, ‘said low the priest,
		‘So may the gods, for thy sake, save thy father !’   	732
She shivered as he spoke, but, lips firm - prest 
	Imprisoning all the anguish at her heart, 
	She filled the fatal cup, 
		Raised her sad eyes, and vaguely gazed around her.	736
Sudden those eyes took light and joy and soul, 
	Sudden from neck to temples flushed the rose, 
	And with quick, gliding steps, 
		And the strange looks of one who walks in slumber,	740
She passed along the floors, and stooped above
	A form, that, as she neared, with arms outstretched, 
	On bended knees sunk down 
		And took the wine - cup with a hand that trembled: 	744
A form of youth - and nobly beautiful 
	As Dorian models for Ionian gods. 
	‘Again!’ it murmured low, 
		‘O dream, at last! at last! how I have missed theel’ 	748
And she replied, ‘The gods are merciful, 
	Keeping me true to thee when I despaired.
	But now from every guest
		Burst the quick cry of anger and of terror;	752
For rose the kneeler, rose that sovereign front 
	Dire to the lives of men as Mars, the star; 
	Zariades the Mede!
		Rang from each lip: flashed from, each sheath the sabre:	756
Thrice stamped the Persian’s foot: to the first sound 
	Ten thousand bucklers echoed back a clang; 
	The next, and the huge walls 
		Shook with the war-shout of ten thousand voices;   	760
The third, and as between divided cloud 
	Flames fierce with deathful pest an angry sun, 
	The folds, flung rudely back, 
		Disclosed behind one glare of serried armour.         	764
On either side, the Persian or the Scyth, 
	The single lord of life and death to both, 
	Stayed, by a look, vain strife;
		And passing onward amid swords uplifted, 	768
A girl’s slight form beside him his sole guard, 
	He paused before the footstool of the King, 
	And in such tones as soothe 
		The wrath of injured fathers, said submissive -	772
I have been guilty to the gods and thee 
	Of man’s most sinful sin, - ingratitude; 
	That which I pined for most 
		Seen as a dream, my waking life rejected; 	776
Now On my knees that blessing I implore.
	Give me thy daughter; but a son receive, 
	And blend them both in one 
		As the mild guardian of the Scythian River.’	780

A BALLAD
By
OLIVER GOLDSMITH
		 “	Turn, gentle hermit of the dale,
	And guide my lonely way, 
To where yon taper cheers the vale. 
	With hospitable ray.
		 “	For here, forlorn and lost tread, 
	With fainting steps and slow; 
Where .wilds immeasurably spread, 
	Seem lengthening as I go”.
		 “	Forbear, my son,” the hermit cries, 
	“To tempt the dangerous gloom;
For yonder faithless phantom flies 
	To lure thee to thy doom.
		 “	Here to the houselesss child of want,
	My door is open still; 
And tho’ my portion is but scant,
	I give it with good will.
		 “	Then turn to-night, and freely share 
	Whate’er my cell bestows;
My rushy couch, and frugal fare, 
	My blessing and repose.
		 “	No flocks that range the valley free,
	To slaughter I condemn: 
Taught by that power that pities me,
	I learn to pity them.
		 “	But from the mountain’s grassy side
	A guiltless feast I. bring; 
A scrip with herbs and fruits supply’d,
	And water from the prisng.
		 “	Then, pilgrim, turn, thy cares forego;
	All earth-born cares are wrong: 
Man wants but little here below,
	Nor wants that little long.”
			Soft as the dew from heav’n descends,
	His gentle accents fell; 
The modest stranger lowly bends,
	And follows to the cell.
			Far in a wilderness obscure
	The lonely mansion lay, 
A refuge to the neighbouring poor
	And strangers led astray.
			No Stores beneath its humble thatch 
	Requir’d a master’s care;
The wicket opening with a latch, 
	Receiv’d the harmless pair.
			And now when busy crowds retire 
	To take their evening rest,
The hermit trimm’d his little fire, 
	And cheer’d his pensive guest;
			And spread his vegetable store, 
	And gayly prest, and smil’d,
And skill’d in legendary lore. 
	The lingering hours bcguil’d.
			Around in sympathetic mirth 
	Its tricks the kitten tries,
The cricket chirrups in the hearth; 
	The crackling faggot flies.
			But nothing could a charm impart 
	To sooth the stranger’s woe;
For grief was heavy at his heart, 
	And tears began to flow.
			His rising cares the hermit spy’d, 
	With answering care opprest:
		 “	And whence, unhappy youth,” he cry’d, 
	“The sorrows of thy breast ?
		 “	From better habitations spurn’d,
	Reluctant dost thou rove; 
Or grieve for friendship unreturn’d,
	Or unregarded love?
		 “	Alas! the joys that fortune brings
	Are triflingi and decay; 
And those who prize the paltry things,
	More trifling still than they.
		 “	And what is friendship but a name,
	A charm that lulls to sleep; 
A shade that follows wealth or fame,
	But leaves the wretch to weep ?
		 “	And love is still an emptier sound,
	The modern fair one’s jest, 
On earth unseen, or only found
	To warm the turtle’s nest.
		 “	For shame, fond youth, thy sorrows hush, 
	And spurn the sex,” he said:
But, while he spoke, a rising blush 
	His love-lorn guest betray’d.
			Surpriz’d he sees new beauties rise 
	Swift mantling to the view,
Like colours o’er the morning skies, 
	As bright, as transient too.
			The bashful look, the rising breast,
	Alternate spread alarms, 
The lovely stranger stands confest
	A maid in all her charms.
			And “ah, forgive a stranger rude, 
	A wretch forlorn,” she cry ‘d,
		 “	Whose feet unhallowed thus intrude 
	Where heaven and you reside.
		 “	But let a maid thy pity share. 
	Whom love has raught to stray;
Who seeks for rest, but finds despair 
	Companion of her way.
		 “	My father liv’d beside the Tyne,
	A wealthy lord was he; 
And all his wealth was Vark’d as mine,
	He had but only me.
		 “	To win me from his tender arms.
	Unnumber’d suitors came; 
Who prais’d me for imputed charms,
	And felt or feign’d flame.
		 “	Each hour a mercenary crowd
	With richest proffers strove,
Amongst the rest young Edwin bow’d, 
	But never talk’d of love.
***

mayilai cīṉi vēṅkaṭacāmi 
āyvuk kaḷañciyam
tokuti varicaikaḷ
tokuti - 1	:	paṇṭait tamiḻaka varalāṟu:  
		cērar - cōḻar - pāṇṭiyar
		ittokutiyil caṅka kāla tamiḻ maṉṉarkaḷ kuṟitta ilakkiyam maṟṟum āvaṇaṅkaḷ cārnta varalāṟṟu āyvukaḷ iṭampeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ipporuḷil  taṉi nūlāka ippōtutāṉ tokukkappaṭukiṉṟatu. palvēṟu nūlkaḷil iṭampeṟṟavai iṅku orucēra uḷḷaṉa. 
tokuti - 2	:	paṇṭait tamiḻaka varalāṟu:  
koṅku nāṭu - pāṇṭiyar - pallavar -  ilaṅkai varalāṟu
		paṇṭait tamiḻakattil koṅku pakuti taṉit taṉmai yōṭu viḷaṅkiya pakutiyākum. mayilai cīṉi. vēṅkaṭa cāmi avarkaḷ koṅku pakutikaḷ kuṟittu ceyta āyvukaḷ iṅku tokukkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. pallava maṉṉarkaḷ paṟṟit taṉittaṉiyāṉa nūlkaḷai mayilai cīṉi avarkaḷ eḻutiṉār. ivaṟṟiliruntu maṉṉarkaḷ kuṟittu varalāṟukaḷ maṭṭum  ittokutiyil tokukkap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. pāṇṭiyarkaḷ kuṟitta takavalkaḷum ittokutiyil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷatu. ilaṅkai tamiḻar varalāṟu kuṟittup pala iṭaṅkaḷil mayilai cīṉi avarkaḷ eḻutiyuḷḷavai ittokutiyil iṇaikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. 
tokuti - 3	:	paṇṭait tamiḻaka varalāṟu: 
kaḷappirar - tuḷu nāṭu
		iruṇṭa kālam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭa varalāṟṟil oḷi pāycciya āyvu kaḷappirar paṟṟiya āyvu ākum. iṉṟaiya karnāṭakap pakutiyiluḷḷa tuḷu nāṭu paṟṟiyum ivar eḻutiyuḷḷār. ivviraṇṭu nūlkaḷum it tokutiyil iṭampeṟukiṉṟaṉa.
tokuti - 4	:	paṇṭait tamiḻakam:
vaṇikam - nakaraṅkaḷ maṟṟum  paṇpāṭu
		mayilai cīṉi palvēṟu taruṇaṅkaḷil eḻutiya paṇṭait tamiḻarkaḷiṉ vaṇikam, paṇṭait tamiḻaka nakaraṅkaḷ maṟṟum palvēṟu paṇpāṭṭuc ceytikaḷ ittokutiyil tokukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
tokuti - 5	:	paṇṭait tamiḻakam: 
āvaṇam - pirāmi eḻuttukaḷ - naṭukaṟkaḷ
		tamiḻarkaḷiṉ tolleḻuttiyal toṭarpāṉa āyvukaḷ tamiḻil mikakkuṟaivē. kaḷaāyvu mūlam mayilai cīṉi avarkaḷ kaṇṭaṟinta pirāmi eḻuttukkaḷ maṟṟum naṭukaṟkaḷ toṭarpāṉa āyvukaḷ ittokutiyil cērkkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
tokuti - 6	:	paṇṭait tamiḻ nūlkaḷ:  
kālaārāycci  - ilakkiya ārāycci
		paṇṭait tamiḻ nūlkaḷiṉ kālam paṟṟiya palvēṟu muraṇpaṭṭa āyvukaḷ tamiḻil nikaḻntuḷḷaṉa. mayilai cīṉi avarkaḷ tamatu kaṇṇōṭṭattil tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ kuṟittuc ceytu kāla āyvukaḷ it tokutiyil tokukkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻk kāppiyaṅ kaḷ maṟṟum pala ilakkiyaṅkaḷ kuṟittu mayilai cīṉi avarkaḷ ceyta āyvukaḷum it tokutiyil iṭam peṟukiṉṟaṉa.
tokuti - 7	:	tamiḻakac camayaṅkaḷ: 
camaṇam
		‘camaṇamum tamiḻum’ eṉṟa poruḷil mayilai cīṉi avarkaḷ eḻutiya nūl ittokutiyil iṭampeṟu kiṉṟatu. camaṇam kuṟittu ivar eḻutiya vēṟu pala kaṭṭuraikaḷ it tokutiyil iṇaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. 
tokuti - 8	:	tamiḻakac camayaṅkaḷ:
pauttam
		‘pauttamum tamiḻum’ eṉṉum poruḷil ivar eḻutiya āyvukaḷ ittokutiyil iṭampeṟukiṉṟaṉa. pauttam toṭarpāka palvēṟu iṭaṅkaḷil mayilai cīṉi eḻutiya kaṭṭuraikaḷ aṉaittum ittokuti yil iṇaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
tokuti - 9	:	tamiḻil camayam: 
kautamaputtariṉ vāḻkkai
		puttariṉ varalāṟu putta jātakak kataikaḷai aṭippaṭai yākak koṇṭu eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ivvakaiyil mayilai cīṉi avarkaḷ eḻutiya puttariṉ vāḻkkai varalāṟu it tokutiyil iṭampeṟṟuḷḷatu. 
tokuti - 10	:	tamiḻil camayam:
pauttak kataikaḷ - icaivāṇar kataikaḷ
		1940 mutal pauttak kataikaḷai tamiḻil moḻi peyarttavar mayilai cīṉi. pauttak kalaivāṇarkaḷiṉ vāḻkkai varalāṟṟaiyum ivar tokuttuḷḷār. iv viraṇṭu nūlkaḷum ittokutiyil iṭampeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. 
tokuti - 11	:	tamiḻil camayam:
putta jātakak kataikaḷ
		putta jātakak kataikaḷ tamiḻil palarāl moḻipeyarkkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. mayilai cīṉi avarkaḷ ceytuḷḷa putta jātakak kataikaḷiṉ moḻipeyarppu ittokutiyil iṭampeṟukiṟatu.
tokuti - 12	:	tamiḻakak kalai varalāṟu:
ciṟpam - kōyil
		mayilai cīṉi tamiḻaka ciṟpaṅkaḷ maṟṟum kōyilkaḷ kuṟittu virivāṉa āyvukaḷai mēṟkoṇṭuḷḷār. palvēṟu iṭaṅkaḷil iṭampeṟṟuḷḷa avvāyvu kaḷai ittokutiyil orucērat tokuttuḷḷōm.
tokuti - 13	:	tamiḻaka kalai varalāṟu: 
		icai - ōviyam - aṇikalaṉkaḷ
		tamiḻarkaḷiṉ paṇmarapu kuṟittum tamiḻ nāṭṭu ōviyam kuṟittum virivāṉa āyvai ivar mēṟkoṇṭuḷḷār. ivvāyvukaḷ aṉaittum ittokutiyil tokukkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. tamiḻarkaḷiṉ aṇikalaṉ kuṟittu mayilai cīṉi. eḻutiyuḷḷa āyvukaḷum ittokutiyil iṭam peṟukiṉṟaṉa.
tokuti - 14	:	tamiḻaka āvaṇaṅkaḷ: 
cācaṉac ceyyuḷ - ceppēṭukaḷ - kalveṭṭukaḷ
		kalveṭṭukkaḷil  ceyyuḷkaḷ mikutiyāka eḻutap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivai aṉaittaiyum tokuttu cācaṉac ceyyuḷ mañcari eṉṟa oru nūlai mayilai cīṉi avarkaḷ veḷiyiṭṭārkaḷ. tamiḻaka varalāṟu toṭarpāka ceppēṭukaḷil kāṇappaṭum virivāṉa takavalkaḷ paṟṟi ivar āyvu ceytuḷḷār. tamiḻil uḷḷa kalveṭṭukkaḷ toṭarpākavum mayilai cīṉi avarkaḷuṭaiya āyvukaḷ kuṟippiṭattakkavai. ivai aṉaittum ittokutiyil iṇaikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. 
tokuti - 15	:	tamiḻaka āvaṇaṅkaḷ: 
maṟaintupōṉa tamiḻ nūlkaḷ
		maṟaintupōṉa tamiḻnūlkaḷ eṉṉum peyaril mayilai cīṉi avarkaḷ nūl oṉṟai eḻutiyuḷḷār. cumār 250 maṟaintu pōṉa nūlkaḷ paṟṟiya virivāṉa takavalkaḷai innūlil avar tokuttuḷḷār. annūl ittokutiyil iṇaikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
tokuti - 16	:	tamiḻ ilakkiya varalāṟu: 
pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭu
		pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭu tamiḻ ilakkiyam eṉṉum peyaril mayilai cīṉi avarkaḷ eḻutiya nūl ittokutiyil iṭampeṟukiṟatu. ipporuḷ kuṟittu tamiḻil uḷḷa ariya nūl ituveṉṟu kūṟumuṭiyum.
tokuti - 17	:	tamiḻ ilakkiya varalāṟu: 
kiṟittuvamum tamiḻum
		airōppiya iyēcu capaikaḷ mūlamāka tamiḻakattiṟku varukaippurinta pātiriyārkaḷ tamiḻukku ceyta toṇṭu aḷaviṭaṟpālatu. ippaṇikaḷ aṉaittai yum ivar tokuttuḷḷār; maṟṟum tamiḻil accuk kalaimūlam uruvāṉa palvēṟu putiya viḷaivukaḷ kuṟittum mayilai cīṉi eḻutiyuḷḷār. ivai aṉaittum ittokutiyil tokukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
tokuti - 18	:	tamiḻiyal āyvu: 
collāyvukaḷ - vāḻkkai varalāṟu
		palvēṟu taruṇaṅkaḷil paṇpāṭṭuc ceytikaḷukkut taravāka coṟkaḷ amaivatu kuṟittu mayilai cīṉi avarkaḷ āyvu ceytuḷḷār. ivvāyvukaḷ it tokutiyil iṇaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. pala aṟiñarkaḷil vāḻkkai varalāṟu kuṟittum ivar eḻutiyuḷḷār ac ceytikaḷum ittokutiyil iṇaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
tokuti - 19	:	patippu, moḻipeyarppu, urai: 
nēminātam - nantikalampakam -  piṟa
		nēminātam, nantikalampakam ākiyavaṟṟai ivar patippittuḷḷār. ‘mattavilācam’ eṉṉum camaskiruta nāṭaka nūlai moḻipeyarttuḷḷār. innūlkaḷ it tokutiyil iṇaikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. mayilai nēmi nātar patikam eṉṟa oru nūlaiyum ivar patippit tuḷḷār. annūl ittokutiyil iṭampeṟukiṟatu. uṇavu muṟaikaḷ kuṟittu ivar eḻutiya uḷḷa nūlum ittokutiyil iṇaikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
tokuti - 20	:	patippu: 
maṉōṉmaṇiyam nāṭakam
		maṉōṉmaṇiyam cuntarappiḷḷai eḻutiya  maṉōṉ maṇiya nāṭakattai mayilai cīṉi patippittuḷḷār. in nāṭakam kuṟitta virivāṉa āyvuraiyaiyum innūlil ceytuḷḷār. innūl ittokutiyil iṭampeṟukiṟatu. 

vī. aracu

kuṟippukaḷ
kuṟippukaḷ
kuṟippukaḷ

ciṅkātaṉam - ciṅkācaṉam. tikaḻtara - viḷaṅka. aṭiyiṉai - iraṇṭu pātaṅkaḷ. kaṭimalar - maṇamuḷḷa pū. āram - cantaṉam; pūmālai. kaḻaikaṟi - karumpaik kuṟikkum. koyyuḷai - koyyappaṭṭa (kattarikkappaṭṭa) piṭari mayir.
jaya vijayīparava-veṟṟi uṇṭāvatāka. tavarājēntirā tava rājarkaḷukku intiraṉ pōṉṟavaṉē. pavam-piṟam irintiṭa muṟintupōka. pīṭu-perumai. 

atta talaiva kēṇmō - kēṭpīrāka, vātavūrar - tiruvātavūriṟ piṟantavar; māṇikkavācakar. “teṉpāṇṭi nāṭē civalōkam” māṇikkavācakar tiruvākku. “tillaiyuḷ kūttaṉē teṉpāṇṭi nāṭṭāṉē”, “mīḷā aruḷpurivāṉ nāṭu eṉṟum teṉpāṇṭi nāṭē teḷi” eṉṟu tiruvācakattil kūṟappaṭṭiruppatu kāṇka.
48 mutal 51 varaiyil uḷḷa aṭikaḷil, nilaulakattaip pacuvākavum, paratakaṇṭattai (intiyā tēcattai appacuviṉ maṭiyākavum, teṉkōṭiyākiya pāṇṭināṭṭai maṭiyil uḷḷa kāmpākavum uruvakam ceykiṟār.
taraṇi - pūmi; nilam. paratam - pārata tēcam, caratam - uṇmai. curai-kāmpu, curattalaiyuṭaiyatu eṉṉum poruḷ uḷḷatu. kaṇṇutal-neṟṟik kaṇṇaiyuṭaiya civaperumāṉ. paṉivarai-imayamalai. tulai-tarācu.
kuṟumuṉi - akattiya muṉivar. malayam - malayamalai, poṉvarai - imaya malai. 55 mutal58 varaiyil uḷḷa aṭikaḷ purāṇak kataiyaik kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa. ataṉaip purāṇakkatai viḷakkattiṟ kāṇka.) 
	cannu - oru muṉivar peyar. ivar kātu vaḻiyākak kaṅkai veḷippaṭṭatu. (ataṉaip purāṇakkatai viḷakkattiṟ kāṇka.) kāḻ akil - vairam poruntiya akil maram. cāṭi - mōti, kuṅkumam - kuṅkuma maram. paṇai - vayal. vāram - ōram. ēyum-okkum.  aṭaiyalar - pakaivar.  kaṭcevi - pāmpu. cuṭikai - uccik koṇṭai. mañcu - mēkam. iñci - matil. uriñci-urāyntu,  utayaṉ - cūriyaṉ.  patākai - koṭi. 

uvāmati - muḻunilā.  mācu - kaḷaṅkam. 79, 80 - ām aṭikaḷ mīṉ uruvam eḻutappaṭṭa pāṇṭiyaṉuṭaiya koṭi kāṟṟil acaintāṭuvatu, muḻu nilāviṉuṭaiya kaḷaṅkattai nakkuvatu  pōlirukkiṟatu eṉṉum karuttuḷḷatu. mīṉkaḷ pāci mutaliya aḻukkait tiṉpatu iyal pākaliṉ, nilāvil uḷḷa kaḷaṅkattai nakkuvatupōlirukkiṟatu eṉṟu kūṟiṉār.

eyiliṉam - kōṭṭaiyiṉ matil, kottaḷam mutaliyavai. entirap paṭai, tantirak karuvi, poṟikaḷ - pakaivar pōrukku vantāl avarkaḷai etirppataṟkākak kōṭṭaic cuvarkaḷiṉmēl amaikkappaṭum pōrkkaruvikaḷ. kiṟikaḷ - vañcaka vaḻikaḷ,   tuṉṉalar - pakaivar.  veruvuvar - añcuvārkaḷ.    porutu - pōrceytu,  araṇkōṭṭai. purai - kuṟṟam; palaviṉam.  muṉamuṉam - mutaṉ mutalil.  āyaṟpāṟṟu -ārāyattakkatu.

parumaṇal iṭṭa katai: itu kōmuṭṭi pāl ūṟṟiṉa katai pōṉṟatu. cila vaḻippōkkar cērntu oṉṟākac cōṟu camaikka eṇṇiṉar. ulaiyil avaravar paṅku ariciyaip pōṭavēṇṭi yirukka, ovvoruvarum maṇalaip peyta kataiyaik kuṟikkiṟatu.

172- 186 variyiṉ karuttu: taṉakkāka maṭṭum vāḻkiṟa mirukat taṉmai yuḷḷa maṉakkōṭṭattai nimirttip piṟar iṉpa tuṉpaṅkaḷaiyum taṉateṉak karutuvataṉālē,  paiyap paiya maṉattai virivaṭaiyac ceytu,  poṟumaiyaiyum aṉpaiyum paṭippaṭiyāka ūṭṭi,  aṟivu teḷintu nāṉ eṉatu eṉṉum cuyanalattai aṭakki eṅkum niṟaintu pēriṉpamāy niṉṟa pēriṉpa veḷḷattil mūḻkac ceytu yāvaraiyum pakkuvappaṭuttukiṟa pāṭacālaiyāka iruppatu illaṟa vāḻkkai eṉpatai aṟiyāmal, tiṉavukoṇṭa mirukaṅkaḷ urāyntu tiṉavu tīrttukkoḷḷum marakkaṭṭai pōla, maṭat taṉattiṉāl tammaiyum piṟaraiyum keṭukkum maṉitariṉ keṭumati eṉṉē eṉpatu.

65 mutal 72 varikaḷil kōṭṭaikkum maṉita uṭampiṟkum uvamai kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. kōṭṭaiyaic cūḻntuḷḷa akaḻi piṟavikkaṭal pōla akalamum, uyirkaḷiṉ āṇavamalam pōṉṟu āḻamum uṭaiyatu; kōṭṭaimatilkaḷ uyirkaḷiṉ aññāṉam pōṉṟu palamuṭaiyaṉa ; matilcuvarkaḷiṉ mēl vaikkappaṭṭuḷḷa pōrk karuvikaḷ aimpulaṉkaḷaip pōl añcattakkaṉa eṉṟa caiva cittānta tattuvaṅkaḷ poruttik kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

appam tiṉṉavō alālkuḻi eṇṇavō eṉpatu, “appam tiṉṉāl pōre kuḻi eṇṇuṉṉen tiṉṉu” eṉṉum malaiyāḷap paḻamoḻiyaik kūṟukiṟatu. intap paḻamoḻiyai “appam tiṉṉāl mati, kutteṇṇeṇṭā” eṉṟum kūṟuvar. kalittuṟai : ulaṇṭu - ulaṇṭu eṉṉum pūcci. uḻalkiṉṟa - piṟappukaḷil cuḻal kiṉṟa. cāṟṟutum - colluvōm. maṉōṉmaṇiyiṉ kātal aṉpu cutta āttumāviṉ ñāṉamākavum, puruṣōttamaṉ avaḷiṭam koṇṭa aṉpu kaṭavuḷiṉ tiruvaruḷākavum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. ñāṉam peṟṟa āṉmāviṭattil īcuvaraṉiṉ karuṇai paṭikiṟatu. eṉṉum tattuvam iṅku oppiṭappaṭukiṟatu.

vari 144 - 145. itu oru paḻamoḻi

vari 162 - 164. ivai mūṉṟum paḻamoḻikaḷ. “kuṟṟamuḷḷa neñcu kuṟukuṟukkum” eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyiṉ karuttuḷḷavai. vitir vitirppu - naṭukkam. taṉṉuḷē - taṉ maṉattiṟkuḷḷē. “koṉṟa piṉ aṉṟō mutalai niṉṟaḻum” eṉpatu āṅkilap paḻamoḻi. mutalaik kaṇṇīr eṉpar.

kaṭinakar - kāval uṭaiya nakaram. vēlai - curaṅkam amaikkum vēlai. tuṉṟu - neruṅkiya. arā - pāmpu. arākkavviya muḻumati - kētu eṉṉum pāmpiṉāl viḻuṅkappaṭṭa muḻu nilā. kētu eṉṉum pāmpu cantiraṉai viḻuṅkuvatāl cantirak kirakaṇam ēṟpaṭukiṟatu eṉpatu purāṇak katai. (itaip purāṇak katai viḷakkattiṟ kāṇka.)
arākkavviya...iḷamukam - vāṇiyiṉ mukam muḻunilā pōṉṟum, piṉṉi viṭappaṭṭa avaḷuṭaiya kūntal cantiraṉai viḻuṅkum pāmpu pōṉṟum irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉpatu karuttu.

13 mutal 25 varikaḷ, ceṭikaḷiṉ iyaṟkai vicittirattaik kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. ceṭikaḷ tamatu pūkkaḷil uḷḷa tēṉaip parukat tēṉī mutaliya pūccikaḷai varac ceytu avaṟṟiṉ mūlamākap pūntātukaḷaik karuppaiyiṟ cērppittuk kāykāyttu vitai yuṇṭākki, ak kāykaḷiṉmēl uḷḷa cuṇaiyiṉāl āṭu māṭu maṉitar paṟavaikaḷ mutaliyavar mūlamāka vevvēṟiṭaṅkaḷil ceṉṟu vaḷarac ceykiṟa tāvaraṅkaḷiṉ iyaṟkai vicittiram kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.

kaṇpaṉippa - kaṇṇīr tuḷikka. cīriya Jḷi - nuṇṇiya maṇal. veṭittu - poṭiyākki. īrttu - iḻuttu, munnīr maṭu - kaṭalākiya nīrnilai. kālattaccaṉ - kālamākiya taccaṉ. cālat takum - peritum poruntum. ōrntu - uṇarntu. aṇṭayōṉi - cūriyaṉ.
34 mutal 59 aṭikaḷ, nīriṉ iyaṟkai vicittirattaik kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. āṟṟil ōṭukiṟa nīr kaṟkaḷai uṭaittup poṭiyākkit taṉṉiṭam akappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷai ellām maṇal kalluṭaṉ aṭittuk koṇṭu pōyk kaṭalil cērkkiṟatu. mīṇṭum an nīrē cūriya veppattiṉāl āviyāka mēlē ceṉṟu mēkamāki maḻaiyākap peytu aruviyākavum āṟākavum cuṉaiyākavum ūṟṟākavum vāykkālākavum ōṭi ōyviṉṟi irāp pakalāka uḻaikkiṉṟatu eṉṉum iyaṟkaiyiṉ vicittirattaik kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.

cāṭu - pāy. īṭṭiyatu - cēkarittatu. ēkuvaṉ - pōvēṉ. eḻili - mēkam. ivaṇ aṭaintu - iṅkē vantu. nirantaram - eppoḻutum. nāṅkūḻp puḻu - maṇpuḻu, nākappūcci. pāṭu - uḻaippu. ōvā - ōyāta. viḻumiya - ciṟanta. yākkai - uṭampu. vētittu - māṟṟi.
65 mutal 80 aṭikaḷ, nāṅkūḻp puḻuviṉ iyaṟkai vicittirattaik kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. paṟavaikaḷ pūccikaḷ taṉṉaip piṭittut tiṉṉātapaṭi

maṇṇil maṟaintu vāḻkiṟa nāṅkūḻp puḻu maṇṇaik kiḷaṟi viṭukiṟatu. ataṉāl kāṟṟum veḷiccamum maṇṇil kalantu maṇ payir paccaikaḷ naṉṟāka vaḷarvataṟku ēṟṟatākiṟatu. aṉṟiyum maṇṇuṭaṉ maṭkippōṉa ilaikaḷaiyum aḻukkukaḷaiyum tiṉṟu jīraṇittu meḻukupōlākki ataic ciṟuciṟu maṇ kaṭṭikaḷāka veḷippaṭutti nilattai urappaṭuttukiṟatu. ivvāṟu payirt toḻilāḷarukku ippuḻu peritum tuṇaipurikiṟa iyaṟkai vicittirattaik kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.   
viḻuppukaḻ - ciṟanta pukaḻ. alakilā - ellai illāta. ilakiya - viḷaṅkiya. ciṟṟāṭi -ciṟiya kaṇṇaṭi; cūriya kiraṇaṅkaḷai oṉṟākac cērttut tīyaiyuṇṭākkukiṟa ciṟu kaṇṇāṭi. maiyam - ciṟṟāṭi yiṉ naṭuviṭam. ciṟṟāṭiyiṉ naṭu maiyam cūriya kiraṇaṅkaḷai oṉṟu cērppatupōla, uṭampiṉ maiyamāka iruppatu maṉam.
87 mutal89 aṭikaḷ, ciṟṟāṭiyiṉ (Hand lens) maiyam cūriya kiraṇaṅkaḷai oṉṟupaṭuttuvatu pōla, uṭampil citaṟiyuḷḷa kuṇaṅkaḷai maṉam oṉṟupaṭuttukiṟatu eṉpataik kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. tīyaṉ - iṅkup palatēvaṉaik kuṟikkiṟatu.
kirupaṇaṉ - ulōpi. tīṉam - eḷiyavaṉ, ēḻai. tōṟṟam - kāṭci. paṭām - tuṇi. potiyil - potikaimalai. mukaṭu - ucci.  poṟ puṟu - aḻakuḷḷa. karu - uruvaṅkaḷai amaikkum vārppaṭa accu (Mould). āṭakap perukku - urukkiya poṉ. arukkaṉ - cūriyaṉ.
95 mutal 98 varikaḷiṉ karuttu: potikaimalaiyiṉmēl cūriyaṉ puṟappaṭukiṟa kāṭci, nāḷ ākiya kammiyaṉ (cūriya veḷiccamākiya) urukkiya poṉṉai (potikai malaiyākiya) accil (mūcaiyil) ūṟṟuvatupōlat tōṉṟukiṟatu eṉpatu.
patākai - koṭi. tokuti - kūṭṭam. aṭupaṭai - kollum cēṉai.

vañcittāḻicai 1. añcalil - añcāta. arikāḷ - pakaivarkaḷē. cañcitam - mikuntuḷḷatu, eñciyuḷḷatu. cañcitap peruvāḻvu - ceṉṟatupōka mītiyuḷḷa vāḻnāḷ.
vañcittāḻicai 2. eñcalil - kuṟaivillāta. mañcuḷa - aḻakuḷḷa. kaṭiñai - piccaip pāttiram.
vañcittāḻicai 3. pitir -mūtātaiyar. tuñciya pitir - iṟantu pōṉa uyirkaḷ. ivarkaḷait teṉpulattār eṉpar. eḷ - iṅku pitirkaḷukku iṭum eḷ. iṟantavarkaḷiṉ cāntikkāka eḷḷum nīrum iṟaippatu intukkaḷiṉ vaḻakkam. jē - jeyam, veṟṟi.

ārppu - āravāram. kaḻal - vīrakkaḻal; veṟṟipeṟṟa pōrvīrar itaik kālil aṇivar. vayappaṭai - valimaiyuḷḷa cēṉai. oḷi - polivu. vañcittoṭai - vañcip pūmālai; māṟṟaracarait tākkuvataṟkākac cellum vīrarkaḷ aṇivatu. itu paḻaiya tamiḻaracariṉ pōr marapu. taṇṇumai poruvum - mattaḷam pōṉṟa. poruppu - malai. urumu - iṭi. ōtai - ōcai. ‘veṇkalap poruppil urumu vīḻnteṉṉa’ eṉpatu veṇkala malaimēl iṭi uruṇṭatu pōla eṉṉum paḻamoḻi. veṇkalak kaṭaiyil yāṉai pukuntatupōla eṉṟuṅ kūṟuvar.
tuṉṉiya - neruṅkiya. kuratta - kuḷampiṉai yuṭaiya. koyyuḷai - kattarikkappaṭṭa piṭari mayir. tiraikkaṭaṟ kūṭṭam - alai vīcukiṉṟa kaṭalpōṉṟa (kutiraikaḷiṉ) kūṭṭam; kutiraip paṭai. peymata maimmukil kūṭṭam - matanīraip poḻiyum karumēkam pōṉṟa (yāṉaikaḷiṉ) kūṭṭam; yāṉaip paṭai. vilōtaṉam - tukilkoṭi. acalattiraḷ - malaikaḷiṉ kūṭṭam. iṅkut tērp paṭaiyaik kuṟikkiṉṟatu.  

vayappaṭai - valimaiyuḷḷa cēṉai. paṭiṟaṉ - vañcakam. camaintāṉ - toṭaṅkiṉāṉ. malayaṉ - malaiya malaikkut talaivaṉ; cēraṉ. aṟaivatu - colluvatu. maritta - cetta.
āmaip palakai - āmaiyiṉ vaṭivamāka amainta maṇai. av vellai - appoḻutu. aṟaivar - colluvār. iyampi - colli. valiyōr - periyōr. itu malaiyāḷa nāṭṭu vaḻakkuc col.
166 mutal 168 aṭikaḷ: periya maṉitariṉ mukak kuṟi, maṉak kuṟikaḷ, avarkaḷ collāmalē avarkaḷ eṇṇiyatait terivikkum eṉṉum karuttuḷḷaṉa.  

cākki - cāṭci, kari. ētuvāka - kāraṇattiṉāl. tetteṭuttal - piḷḷaip pēṟillātavar piṟaruṭaiya piḷḷaiyaic cuvīkāram ceytal. kuṭakaṉ - cēraṉ. uṟutuyar - varukiṟa tuṉpattai. āṟṟār - poṟukkamāṭṭār. paṭiyōr - ulakattavar.
cāṟṟuvar - colluvārkaḷ. tēṟṟam - teḷivu. māṟṟam - col, pēccu. aṉumāṉittal - karutal aḷavai; kāriyattaik kaṇṭu kāraṇattaiyuṇartal. aḷavai - piramāṇam, taṉmai. aṅkāṭi - kaṭaitteru. ampalam - palarum kūṭum iṭam.
209-ām vari. ‘araṇmaṉai irakaciyam aṅkāṭi paraciyam’ eṉṉum malaiyāḷa nāṭṭup paḻamoḻiyaik kuṟikkiṉṟatu.  

antaraṅkap poruḷ - irakaciyaṅkaḷ uḻaiyuḷār - arukil uḷḷavar. aṭaiyavum - muḻuvatum. aṟaikuvam - colluvōm.

naḷḷirā - naṭu iravu. kaiyaṟu nittirai - taṉṉai maṟantu tūṅkutal. vaikaṟai - viṭiyaṟ kālam.   

aravam - ōcai. nitiyeṭuppavarpōl - putaiyal tōṇṭi eṭuppavar pōla.
13 - 15 varikaḷ, araṇmaṉaiyiliruntu āciramam varaiyil muṉivar curaṅkam tōṇṭuvatāl uṇṭākum aravattaik kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa.
19 vari. kaṇṭatu kaṉavō? - nī kātal koḷḷak kāraṇamāyiruntatu kaṉavuk kāṭciyō?
kaṇṇuḷār - kaṇṇilē taṅki yirukkiṟār. cittira rēkai - cittiralēkai. ivaḷ, vāṇācuraṉ makaḷākiya uṣai eṉpavaḷiṉ tōḻi. maṉitar uruvattaic cittiramāka

varaivatil vallavaḷ. uṣai tāṉ kaṉaviṟ kaṇṭu kātal koṇṭa aniruttaṉai iṉṉāṉ eṉṟu aṟiyāmal varuntiya pōtu, cittiralēkai avaṉ uruvattai ōviyamāka eḻutik kāṭṭiṉāḷ. av vuruvantāṉ tāṉ kātalittavaṉ eṉṟu kūṟa, cittiralēkai taṉ māyā jālattiṉāl aniruttaṉai avaṉ uṟaṅkiya kaṭṭilōṭu koṇṭuvantu uṣaiyiṉiṭam viṭṭāḷ eṉpatu purāṇak katai. nī cittiralēkai allai - eṉpatu, cittiralēkaipōla vāṇi ōviyam eḻuta vallavaḷ allāḷ eṉpatu
34 vari. paḻakiyum uḷaiyē - vāṇi taṉ kātalaṉai nēril kaṇṭu paḻaki yirukkiṟāḷ eṉpatum, maṉōṉmaṇi taṉ kātalaṉai nēril kāṇāmal kaṉavil maṭṭum kaṇṭirukkiṟāḷ eṉpatum karuttu.
ceyyuḷ 1 .	aṭavi - kāṭu. pātai viṭuttu - vaḻi tavaṟi. aḷitta - pātukātta.  

ce. 2. varai - aḷavu, ellai. varai kuṟiyātu niḻalai aḷattal - taṉ niḻalai aḷakka virumpiya oruvaṉ, an niḻaliṉ ellaiyaik kuṟikkāmalē aḷakkat toṭaṅkiṉāl atu aḷavukku akappaṭāmal nīṇṭukoṇṭē pōkum eṉpatu. āriruḷ - niṟainta iruḷ. uṭala māṟum vakai - uṭalam āṟum vakai eṉṟum, uṭalam māṟum vakai eṉṟum pirittu iruporuḷ koḷka. vīṭu - illam, mōṭcam.
ce. 3. ēkāntam - taṉitta iṭam. peruṅ kakaṉam - periya veḷi. aṇaiyār - cēra māṭṭārkaḷ. cintai aṟa - maṉam illai yākumpaṭi. pantam - ulakap paṟṟu, kaṭṭu.
ce. 4. paṇip pāy - alaṅkārap paṇi ceyyappaṭṭa pāy, tirumāl paḷḷikoḷḷum pāmpākiya pāy. pūaṇai - malar aṇai; piramaṉ irukkiṟa veṇṭāmaraiyākiya aṇai - uṉṉār - niṉaikka māṭṭārkaḷ.
ce. 5. vētaṉai - tuṉpam. mey - uṭampu. calippu - kaḷaippu. viṭiyum - nīṅkum, pōkum.   
ce. 6. ētō taṉ paḻa niṉaivu eḻa - vaḻippōkkaṉukku muṉivaraippaṟṟi ētō paḻaiya niṉaivu uṇṭāka. kaṇpaṉittu - kaṇṇīr kacintu.
ce. 7. iḻaikka - ceyya. antaram - ākāyam. cuntaram -aḻaku. tārakaikaḷ - viṇmīṉkaḷ. aṟaintu - colli.
ce. 8. piṇainta - cērnta. aṉṟil - aṉṟiṟ paṟavai. ivai āṇum peṇṇum iṇaipiriyāmal irukkum eṉṟum, pirintāl uṭaṉē uyirviṭum eṉṟum kūṟuvar. ivai kaṟpaṉaip paṟavai. alarmalar - alarkiṉṟa malarkaḷ. kaṇṇīr aruvi - kaṇṇīr aruvi eṉṟum, kaḷ (tēṉākiya) nīr aruvi eṉṟum iruporuḷ koḷḷalām. akam uṭaintu - maṉamuṭaintu. tūva - coriya.
ce. 9. vintai - vittai. terikka - colla. viḷippavariṉ - kūppiṭu kiṟavarpōla. vāval - vauvāl paṟavai, vauvāl taṉatu tōl ciṟakukaḷai acaittup paṟappatu kaiyai acaittu aḻaippatu pōla irukkiṟatu. viḷakkoṭu - veḷiccattōṭu.  

anti - mālainēram. ēvi - pōkaviṭṭu. antiyai muṉ ēvi iraveṉṉum iṟaivi vantiṟuttaṉaḷ - mālai nēramākiya tōḻiyai muṉ pōkaviṭṭu iravākiya irāṇi avaḷ piṉṉē vantāḷ.
ce. 10. poriyaravu - puḷḷikaḷaiyuṭaiya pāmpu. cuṭikai - talai, ucci. polaṉmaṇi - polivuḷḷa māṇikka maṇi. pāmpukaḷiṉ talaiyil māṇikka maṇi uṇṭeṉpatu kaviñarkaḷiṉ kaṟpaṉai. oḷi - veḷiccam. tiṇ - tiṇṇiya, palamuḷḷa. kaṟaipaṭi - yāṉai, (uralpōṉṟa kālaiyuṭaiyatu). maruppu - tantam. uḻuvai - puli. aḻal viḻi - neruppup pōṉṟa kaṇkaḷ.
ce. 11. pērayarvu - atikat taḷarcci, maṉam iṟantu - maṉam kaṭantu.
ce. 12. oruṅkuāra - oṉṟāka neruṅkiyuḷḷa. muḷari- muṭkaḷ. uḻai - pakkam. ōṅkāra oli tarum kāṉanati - kāṭṭāṟukaḷiṉ nīroli ōṅkāra cattattai uṇṭākkiṉa.   

ce. 13. muṉivar namatu iruppiṭattai yaṭaintapōtu aṅkē erintukoṇ ṭirunta neruppuc cuṭar, talaiyait tāḻtti varavēṟṟatupōliruntatu.
ce. 14. nimalam - nirmalam, cuttamāṉa. meyppattar - uṇmaiyāṉa paktar.
ce. 15. paṉippu - kuḷir, naṭukkam. palam - paḻam. mūlam - kiḻaṅku.
ce. 16. nāvil - nākkil. oru viralait tāṇṭutal - oru viraṟ kiṭai tūrattaik kaṭattal. nāviṉāl kuṇṭali caktiyai eḻuppi oru viraṟkiṭai tūrattaik kaṭantu amuta kalaiyai uṇṇuvatu haṭayōka muṟai. itaṉai yōkikaḷ ceyvārkaḷ. cākaram - kaṭal. kantam - kiḻaṅku. aṉantam - pala.   

ce. 17. tirunta - cemmaiyāka. uṟaiti - taṅkuvāy. paṉippu - naṭukkam.
ce. 18. iyampiyum - colliyum. icaiyāmal - collāmal. malaivu - mayakkam. pakarntāṉ - coṉṉāṉ.
ce. 19. mukamati orukaip patuma malar maṟaippa - mukamākiya cantiraṉaik kaiyākiya tāmaraiyiṉāl maṟaittāṉ. muṉivaruṭaṉ vanta vālipaṉ, uṇmaiyil peṇmakaḷākai yiṉāl, tīyiṉ veḷiccattil taṉṉaip peṇ eṉṟu terintu koḷḷātapaṭi mukattaik kaiyiṉāl maṟaittāṉ eṉpatu karuttu. cuvar ōviyam - cuvaril eḻutappaṭṭa ōviyam. paṇṭaik kālattil ōviyaṅ kaḷaic cuvarkaḷilē eḻutiṉārkaḷ. karutiṭuvāṉ - eṇṇuvāṉ.
ce. 20. iravu vēḷaiyil, tīccuṭar aṭaṅkuvatum poṅki erivatumāka irunta kāṭci, eliyaip piṭitta pūṉai ataṉaik koṉṟu tiṉpataṟku muṉṉar. avveliyai ōṭaviṭṭup piṭittu viḷaiyāṭuvatupōla iruntatu eṉpatu ic ceyyuḷiṉ karuttu.
ce. 21. vatinta kuṟi - mukattil kāṇappaṭṭa kuṟi. eḻilārum iḷamai - aḻaku poruntiya iḷamaip paruvam. iṭaiyūṟāti - tuṉpam mutaliyavai. ivaṇ - iṅkē.
ce. 22. ōtāy - colluvāy. kaḻaṉṟaṉaḷō - nīṅkiṉāḷō. mey viṭuttu - uṇmaiyai veḷiyē viṭṭu. kaḻaṟāy - colluvāy.
ce. 23. vaiyakam - ulakam. kaiyārum - kaiyil varum. ilavu - ilava maram. ilavaṅkāy paḻukkātu. muṟṟiya piṟaku veṭikkum. paḻuttāl uṇṇalām eṉṟu kāttukkoṇṭirukkum kiḷi ēmāṟṟam aṭaiyum. ākavē ‘ilavukātta kiḷi pōla’ eṉṉum paḻamoḻi vaḻaṅkukiṟatu. ic ceyyuḷiṉ mutal iraṇṭaṭikaḷiṉ karuttu, tī eriyumpōtu anta veḷiccattil poruḷkaḷiṉ niḻal kāṇappaṭṭu atu taṇiyumpōtu niḻalum maṟaintuviṭuvatupōla ulaka cukaṅkaḷ nilaittirā eṉpatu.
ce. 24. “neyuṟu kuṭatteṟumpu naṇṇal” - neyk kuṭattai eṟumpu moyppatu pōla eṉṉum paḻamoḻi.  
muyaṟkompu - ilporuḷ uvamai. peruṅ kapaṭam iṭum kalaṉ - vañcakamākiya poruḷkaḷai iṭṭu vaikkum pāttiram. piṟaṅkum - viḷaṅkum.
ce. 25. aṭikaḷ - muṉivar. ētilaṉ - vaṟiyavaṉ. veyiliṉmuṉ viḷakkoḷi eṉpatu paḻamoḻi. miṉṉeḷiyil kaṇpōla - miṉṉal veḷiccattil kaṇ kūcuvatupōla veḷippaṭṭa kaḷvaṉ - piṭipaṭṭa tiruṭaṉ.
ce. 26. yati - tuṟavi, muṉivar. iṟumpūtu - viyappu. vēṣa rakaciyaṅkaḷ - māṟuvēṭam. veṭṭa veḷiyāṉa - veḷippaṭṭaṉa.
ce. 27. niṉmala vipūti - tūymaiyāṉa tirunīṟu. poṉ maya mey - poṉ niṟamāṉa uṭampu. nīṟupaṭi neruppu - nīṟiyuḷḷa cāmpalukkuḷḷē irukkiṟa neruppu. kaṇ iṇai - iraṇṭu kaṇkaḷ. terikkum - kāṭṭum.
ce. 28. eḻil - aḻaku. kuḻal - kūntal. caṭaikkōla maḵtu - caṭaik kōlattai. vīcu ulaiyiṉ mūkku eṉa - ulaikkaḷattil turutti yiṉ mūkkiliruntu veḷippaṭum kāṟṟaippōla. veytuyirppu - perumūccu.
ce. 29. cemittaruḷvai - poṟuttaruḷka.
ce. 30. kāvirippū mānakar - kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉam. utittōr - piṟantavar.
ce. 31. malaṭi - vanti, piḷḷaippēṟu illātavaḷ. ceppariya - colluvataṟku arumaiyāṉa. cekam - ulakam.
ce. 32. aṭal - valimai. aruḷuvar - aṉpē uruvamāṉavar. alakuil - aḷavillāta. aṟaivaṉō - colluvēṉō.
ce. 33. puḷakam mūṭi - mayirkkūcceṟintu. vataṉam - mukam. iḷampiṭi - iḷaiya peṇyāṉai pōṉṟa peṇ.  
- patiṉāṟu kalaikaḷaiyuṭaiya cantiraṉ, nilā. kālkāṟṟu, pirintirukkum kātalarukku veṇṇilāvum, iḷaṅ kāṟṟum tuṉpam ceykiṟatu eṉpatu karuttu.


ce. 34. maṭamakaḷ - iḷampeṇ. taṭumāṟā - taṭumāṟi. takaimai - nalla kuṇaṅkaḷ.
ce. 35. vētaṉaippaṭṭu - varuttam aṭaintu. paṭiṟu - vañcakam.
ce. 36. polam - aḻaku, poṉ. pukalil - coṉṉāl. aruḷ arumpi - aṉpu mukiḻttu. atipar - talaivar. ayarntiruntēṉ - maṟantiruntēṉ.
ce. 37. uṉṉi - niṉaittu. tiruvārum - aḻakuḷḷa. cēṭiyar - tōḻimār. terippar - colluvārkaḷ.

ce. 38. āyattār - tōḻip peṇkaḷ. āyavum - eṇṇip pārkkavum. akal vēlai - paranta kaṭal. eṟiyum - alaivīcum. akōrāttiram - pakalum iravum. kaṭal ōcai pirinta kātalar kaḷukkut tuṉpam uṇṭākkum eṉpatu kavimarapu. kalaimati  - patiṉāṟu kalaikaḷaiyuṭaiya cantiraṉ, nilā. kālkāṟṟu, pirintirukkum kātalarukku veṇṇilāvum, iḷaṅ kāṟṟum tuṉpam ceykiṟatu eṉpatu karuttu.  
ce. 39. kāciṉi - pūmi. kaṭṭam - kaṣṭam. eṉaitteṉka - evvaḷavu eṉṟu colla.
ce. 40. ollai - viraivil. uṉṉi - eṇṇi. tīkatuva - tī paṟṟi eriya. karantu - maṟaintu, oḷintu.
ce. 41. tīrttakuḷam - puṇṇiya nīrttuṟaikaḷ. mūrtti - mūrttaṅkaḷ uḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷ. talam - iṭam. iṅkuk kōyilkaḷaik kuṟikkiṟatu. tirukkuaṟu - mayakkam nīṅkiya. vātiyar - camayavātam ceyvōr. maṉṟu -capai, ampalam. maṟuttu uṟaṅkum - uṭal tūkkattai maṟuttu yōka nittirai tūṅkum. yōkiyar - yōkam ceypavar. 
ce. 42. māṉ - peṇmāṉ. kalai - āṇmāṉ. piṭi - peṇyāṉai. mā - mirukam. iṅku āṇyāṉaiyaik kuṟikkiṟatu. vaṉpikam - valimaiyāṉa kuyil. cukam - kiḷi. eññāṉṟum - eppōtāvatu. navilvir - colluṅkaḷ. aṉantam - muṭivillāta (pala). ic ceyyuḷukkuk kuṟippupporuḷ: māl (mayakkattai) viṭāta 64 kalaiñāṉaṅkaḷē! piṭivātamē vāḻvākakkoṇṭa matac caccaravu uṭaiya ākulamē! (āravāraṅkaḷē!) anittamākiya jaṭatukka muḷḷa ikalōkaṅkaḷē! cukamuḷḷavai eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟa intira lōkam mutaliya paralōkaṅkaḷē eṉpatu.
ce. 43. kāyam - uṭampu. aruvaruppu - veṟuppu.
ce. 44. puṉitar - cuttamāṉavar. pōtam - aṟivu.  

ce. 46. ciṟcāyai - cila cāyal. vakuttu - viparamākac colli.
ce. 47. ivvaḻalē kati - inta neruppilē viḻuntu iṟappēṉ eṉpatu karuttu. mūturuvam viḷakki - paḻaiya uruvattai veḷippaṭak kāṭṭi. moḻikuḷaṟi - col taṭumāṟi.
ce. 48. aṟaiyum - collum. curuti - iṅkuc cevi vaḻic ceyti eṉpatu poruḷ.
ce. 49. pārvati - civaperumāṉiṉ cakti. pārati - kalaimakaḷ, caracuvati. kañcap pārkkavi - centāmarai malaril eḻuntaruḷiyuḷḷa tirumakaḷ, ilakkumi. aruntati - ivaḷ kaṟpukkut teyvamāka viḷaṅkupavaḷ. aruṅkati - kiṭaittaṟkariya nilai. ārvam - aṉpu. ārka - poruntuka. āraṇaṅkaḷ - vētaṅkaḷ. ārtta - āravāram ceytaṉa.  
ce. 50. āḻi - kaṭal. puṭaicūḻ - pakkaṅkaḷilē cūḻnta. pantaṉaikaḷ - tuṉpaṅkaḷākiya kaṭṭukaḷ. cinta - keṭa. nirantaram - eppoḻutum.
kaḻaṟāy - colvāy. etirccaiyā - taṟceyalāy. kaṭipuri - kāval amainta kōṭṭai. perumalai - iṅkup potikaimalaiyaik kuṟikkiṟatu. ciṟukāl celvaṉ - iḷaṅkāṟṟākiya makaṉ.

teḷnīrk kaṉṉi - teḷinta nīrākiya peṇ. ciṟṟil - ciṟuvīṭu; ciṟumikaḷ viḷaiyāṭṭākak kaṭṭuvatu. kāvi - kuvaḷaippū, nīlat tāmarai. aṭarttu - koytu. cārttilaṉ - cūṭavillai. māṉa - okka, pōla. māṭē - iṭattil.  
pūtapporuḷ - parupporuḷ, stūlap poruḷ. pulaṉ tuṇai - aimpulaṉkaḷiṉ utavi. pōtapporuḷ - aṟivup poruḷ. iravi - cūriyaṉ.
vari 85.  kaṇ cemmaiyāka iruntāl kāṭci naṉṟākat teriyum. paṭalam mutaliya ēṟpaṭṭāl kāṭci naṉṟākat teriyātu eṉpatu karuttu.
tuvakku intiriyam - toṭṭu uṇarum pulaṉ. vaṭakku nōkki - vaṭakku ticaiyaik  kāṭṭum oru yantiram. itil uḷḷa muḷ eppōtum vaṭakku ticaiyilēyē tirumpi niṟkum. pūtayākkai - paruvuṭal. karattāl - kaiyiṉāl.  
ēyppa - oppa, pōla. aṉumāṉa āti - aṉumāṉam pirattiyaṭcam mutaliya aḷavaikaḷ. ayirppōm - cantēkappaṭuvōm.
ārppu - āravāram. vemcaram - koṭiya ampu. viḷampu - collu.
paritapittu - varunti. uṟār - aṭaiyamāṭṭār. emaṉ - cāvu. kurūrak kūṟṟiṉ - koṭumaiyuḷḷa emaṉuṭaiya. virūpam - avalaṭcaṇam. cilanti - cilantip pūcci. pacai aṟu - irakkam illāta. al - iravu, iruṭṭu. pēḻ - periya. verukkoḷa - añcumpaṭi. karaṅkaḷ - cilantiyiṉ kālkaḷ.

b vantuṟa - mutuku poruntumpaṭi. icittu - iḻuttu; itu koccaic col eṉṟu cilar ikaḻvar. iccol kuṇṭalakēci kāviyattilum payilappaṭṭuḷḷatu. (puṟattiraṭṭu 411-ām ceyyuḷ kāṇka.) kaṟikka - kaṭikka. eyyātu - oḻiyāmal pañcā catkōṭi - aimpatu kōṭi.
28-29 varikaḷ, intap pūmi aimpatukōṭi yōcaṉai parappuḷḷatu eṉṉum purāṇak karuttaik kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.
iraviyiṉ maṇṭalam - cūriya maṇṭalam; cūriya maṇṭalattil pala aṇṭaṅkaḷ aṭaṅkiyuḷḷaṉa. mīṉiṉam - naṭcattirat tokuti. alakilai - aḷavillātatu. oppaṟu - oppillāta. unti - koppūḻ. muḷari - tāmarai.
nāṉmukac cilanti - nāṉku mukamuṭaiya piramaṉākiya cilanti nāṟṟiya - kaṭṭit toṭaṅkaviṭṭa. mālum - tirumālum. pēyttēr - kāṉalnīr. makōtati - peruṅkaṭal. puṟputam - nīril tōṉṟum kumiḻi. intirajālam iv entira vicēṭam - inta ulakam māyaiyiṉ kāriyamāka amaintatu. tirikai - iyantirak kal. ārkkum - iraikiṉṟa. mīkkoḷum - atikappaṭum.
vari - 67. ‘caṭṭicuṭṭatu kaiviṭṭatu’ eṉpatu paḻamoḻi.  

pirattiyak pirapōtayam - uḷmukamāṉa meyññāṉam. akaṇṭa - paranta. citkaṉam - aṟivumayam. nirupātikam - kālatēca vivakāraṅkaḷaik kaṭantuniṟkum nilai. 76-78 varikaḷ. pāliṉ niṟattaiyaṟiyāta piṟavik kuruṭaṉukkup pāliṉ niṟam kokkup pōṉṟatu eṉṟu coṉṉa kataiyaik kuṟikkiṟatu. (katai viḷakkat tiṟ kāṇka.) cutamām - tāṉē teriyum. anupūti - tāṉ aṟintatum āṉāl piṟarukkuc colla muṭiyātatumāṉa aṟivu. mōṉam - mauṉam. citti - mōṭcam. mutti - mōṭcam. puraiyaṟu - kuṟṟamaṟṟa. kuravaṉ - kurunātaṉ.  
uñaṟṟi - ceytu. ciṉakaram - kōyil. ic col mutalil jaiṉak kōvilukkup peyarāka amaintu, piṟaku kōyil eṉṉum potup poruḷaip peṟṟatu. akam uṭaintu - maṉam varunti.
viṭum paricu - viṭukiṟa vitam. kaiyaṟum - ceyalaṟṟirukkum. kaṇita caṅkētam - kaṇakkuk kuṟiyīṭu. karatalā malakam, karatala + āmalakam - kaiyil nellikkaṉi. ‘uḷḷaṅkai nellikkaṉi.’ 
viṇṭiṭā - piṇṭupōkātu. vīkkiya - kaṭṭiya. pācam - kayiṟu. ākaruṣṇam - iḻukkum cakti. yāppuuṟṟu - kaṭṭuṇṭu. payiṟṟiṭu - payiṟci aḷikkiṉṟa. kuyiṟṟiya - ceyta. pūriya - eḷiya. akkaram - aṭcaram, eḻuttu.

pāricātam - pavaḻamallippū. itu iravil malarvatu. 179-181 varikaḷ: veṇṇiṟa malarkaḷ irāk kālattil malarvataṉ kāraṇam, iruṭṭil veṇṇiṟam kāṭṭi vaṇṭukaḷaiyum īkkaḷaiyum kavarcci ceyvataṟkāka eṉpatu. ucitam - poruttam. nici - irāttiri. ceṟivu - aṭarcci, nerukkam. uṟukaṇ - tuṉpam. ētu - kāraṇam. ilakātatu - viḷaṅkātatu, teriyātatu. icaivu - poruttam.  
aṭikaḷ - cuntaramuṉivar. curuṅkai - karantupaṭai. oruttarum aṟiyātapaṭi maṟaittu amaikkappaṭṭa vaḻi. nīr pōvataṟku amaikkappaṭṭu, atu teriyātapaṭi mēlē mūṭappaṭṭa kālvāykkum peyar. curuṅkai eṉpatu kirēkka moḻi. ki. mu. mutal nūṟṟāṇ ṭilum ki. pi. 1, 2 nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷilum kirēkkarākiya yavaṉar tamiḻ nāṭṭuṭaṉ vāṇikat toṭarpu koṇṭirunta kālattil, avar mūlamākat tamiḻmoḻiyil kalanta kirēkkac coṟkaḷil ituvum oṉṟu. iccol caṅka kālattu nūlkaḷilum piṟkālattu. nūlkaḷilum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. toṭṭiṭa - tōṇṭa. (toṭu - tōṇṭu.)

eyppiṉil vaippu - iḷaitta kālattil utavuvataṟku vaitta poruḷ, cēmaniti. kaippaṭu kaṉi - uḷḷaṅkai nellikkaṉi. camucayam - aiyam. iḻutu - ney. camāti - piramattōṭu maṉam oṉṟu paṭṭirukkum nilai. araṟṟi - vāyviṭṭuk kataṟi.  

mukamaṉ - muṉniṉṟu pārāṭṭuvatu. utayam - uṟappāṭu. camayikaḷ - pala matattārkaḷ.

kaṭampāṭu - kaṭamai. vīraruṭaiya kaṭamai, ceñcōṟṟuk kaṭaṉ kaḻippatu.
pīṭai - tuṉpam. vākaṉam - (iṅku) pallakku, civikai. alaku ilā - eṇṇikkai illāta. kaḷam - iṭam. ‘mīṉ uṇṇak kuḷakkarai irukkum kokkeṉa’ eṉpatu ‘kokkokkak kūmpum paruvattu; maṟṟataṉ, kuttokka cīrtta iṭattu’ eṉṟa tirukkuṟaḷ karuttu. campavam - nikaḻcci. caṅkati - toṭarpu, ceyti. ‘eṇṇār tuṇintapiṉ paṇṇār tāmatam’ eṉpatu, ‘eṇṇit tuṇika karumam; tuṇintapiṉ, eṇṇuvam eṉpatu iḻukku’ eṉṟa tirukkuṟaḷiṉ karuttaik kūṟukiṟatu.

aṭaiyavum - muḻuvatum. itakkeṭu - naṉmaik kēṭāṉatu.

kuṟuvāḷ - ciṟuvāḷ. pakaḻi. - ampu, tūṇi - ampukaḷai iṭṭu vaikkum tūṇi. nāṇniṉṟu - villiṉ nāṇiliruntu. urumu - iṭi. kāṟ paṭaiyāḷar - kālāḷ paṭaiyiṉar. kaliṉam - kaṭivāḷam. ‘karuttum viraivu’ - maṉōvēkam. kuram - kutiraiyiṉ kuḷampu. kūṉ piṟai - vaḷainta nilāppiṟai pōṉṟa. tuṇai maruppu - iraṇṭu tantaṅkaḷ. mummatam - mūṉṟu matanīr. yāṉaikaḷukku mūṉṟuvita matanīr perukuvatāl mummatam eṉap peyarpeṟṟatu. vāraṇap puyal iṉam - mēkam pōṉṟu karuniṟamuḷḷa yāṉaik kūṭṭam. venniṭ ṭōṭiya - mutuku kāṭṭi ōṭiṉa. purantaraṉ - intiraṉ. ‘muṉṉoru vaḻutikku venniṭṭōṭiya parantaraṉ’ eṉpatu, pāṇṭiyaṉ oruvaṉ intiraṉuṭaṉ pōr ceytu veṉṟa kataiyaik kuṟikkiṟatu. ik kataiyait tiruviḷaiyāṭaṟ purāṇam, intiraṉ muṭimēl vaḷai eṟinta paṭalattil kāṇka. purantaraṉ kaippaṭāp poruppukaḷ - intiraṉuṭaiya kaivāḷiṉāl ciṟakai iḻakkāta malaikaḷ. muṉ kālattil malaikaḷ ciṟaku peṟṟu vāṉattil paṟantu tirintaṉa eṉṟum, intiraṉ avaṟṟiṉ ciṟakukaḷai veṭṭi vīḻttiyapaṭiyāl, anta malaikaḷ paṟakka muṭiyāmal nilattil taṅkiviṭṭaṉa eṉṉum purāṇakkataiyaik kuṟikkiṟatu inta aṭi.
tērkaḷ malaikaḷukku uvamai. koṭiñci neṭuntēr - koṭiñci eṉṉum uṟuppaiyuṭaiya periya tēr. nāṟpaṭai - nāṉku vitamāṉa paṭai. avai, yāṉaip paṭai, kutiraippaṭai, tērp paṭai, kālāṭpaṭai eṉpaṉa. yākkai - uṭampu. pāṇṭi - pāṇṭi nāṭākiya tāy. mutunati mātā - tāmpiravarṇi āṟākiya tāy.

cōri - irattam. poṉṟiṭum aḷavum - cākiṟa varaiyil. vintam aṭakkiyōṉ - vintiya malaiyait tāḻac ceytavaṉākiya akattiyaṉ. muṟkālattil vintiya malai uyarntu iṟumāp paṭaintiruntatu eṉṟum, avvaḻiyāka vanta akattiya muṉivar ataṉ talaiyil taṉ kaiyai vaittuc ciṟitāka aṭakkiṉār eṉṟum purāṇam kūṟam. tantanaṟṟamiḻmoḻi - akattiyaṉ mutalil ilakkaṇam eḻuti amaikkappaṭṭa tamiḻmoḻi. kiḻamai - urimai. ōttaṟuttu - tāḷam piṭittu.

kayavar - kīḻmakkaḷ. intaḷam - viṟaku, erikarumpu, tiruntalīr - pakaivarkaḷē. jayamātu - jayalaṭcumi. irantu kōṭṭakkatu - vēṇṭikkoḷḷum takutiyuṭaiyatu. ‘purantārkaṇ ṇīrmalkac cākiṟpiṟ cākkāṭu, irantukōṭ ṭakka tuṭaittu’ eṉṉum tirukkuṟaḷuṭaṉ ivvaṭiyai oppunōkkuka.

utiyaṉ - cēraṉ. uṟṟu - aṭaintu, vantu. aṟṟam - camayam. teruḷ - teḷivu, viḷakkam. aṭal - valivu, vīram toṭai - mālai. māṟṟam - col, pēccu. kumpam - kuṭam. nimpa mālai - vēppa mālai; itu pāṇṭiya maṉṉarukku uriyatu.
matil tiṟam - kōṭṭaiyiṉ valimai. maṭpari - maṇkutirai. natiyiṭai maṭpari naṭāttiṉōr - eṉpatu ‘maṇ kutiraiyai nampi āṟṟil iṟaṅkiṉatu pōla’ eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyaik kuṟikkiṟatu. oṟuttiṭa - taṇṭikka, uḻiñai - uḻiñaip pūmālai. paṇṭaikkālattut tamiḻaracar, pakaiyaracariṉ kōṭṭaiyai muṟṟukai ceyyumpōtu aṇiyum mālai. kōṭṭaiyai vaḷaittu muṟṟukai iṭuvataṟku  uḻiñaip pōr eṉpatu peyar. uḻiñai cūṭuvaṉ - kōṭṭaiyai muṟṟukaiyiṭuvāṉ. avāviya -ācaippaṭṭa, curumpu ār - vaṇṭukaḷ moykkiṟa. vitukulam- cantira kulam  (vitu - cantiraṉ )

nocci - nocci mālai: kōṭṭaiyaic cūḻntu muṟṟukaiyiṭu vōraik kōṭṭaiyiṉ uḷḷiruntu pōr ceyvōr aṇiyum mālai. kōṭṭaikkuḷḷiruntu pakaivarai etirkkum pōrukku noccip pōr eṉpatu peyar. ētam - kuṟṟam. olkalai - taḷarātē. uravōr - maṉavali yuṭaiyavar. umpal - yāṉai.
265.	inta aṭi, “citaiviṭat tolkār uravōr putaiyampiṉ, paṭṭuppā ṭūṉṟuṅ kaḷiṟu” eṉṉum tirukkuṟaḷiṉ karuttuḷḷatu

271-m aṭi. “nāṇakattillā riyakkam marappāvai, nāṇāl uyir maruṭṭiyaṟṟu” eṉṉum tirukkuṟaḷiṉ karuttuḷḷatu.
272 - 273 aṭikaḷ, “oṭṭār piṉceṉ ṟoruvaṉ vāḻtaliṉ an nilaiyē, keṭṭāṉ eṉappaṭutal naṉṟu” eṉṉum tirukkuṟaḷaik kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. tappiṉāy irumuṟai - pōrkkaḷattilum, taṟkolai ceyya niṉaittapōtum aracaṉ tappiyatu. tappili - piḻai illātavaṉ eṉṟum pōkkiri eṉṟum iruporuḷ koḷka.
piriyappaṭukai - aṉpu  celuttuvatu, kāmaṅ koḷvatu miṇṭalai - pēcātē. miṇṭuka eṉpatum cēra nāṭṭil vaḻaṅku kiṟa malaiyāḷa moḻic col.
taiyalar - peṇkaḷ. iṅkuk kaṉṉimāṭattil irukkum peṇkaḷaic cuṭṭukiṟatu. vēṟarak kaḷaikuvam - eṉṟatu nārāyaṇaṉaik karutik kūṟiyatu.

ātiyar - ciṟantavar. aṭi 24 - 25: ‘vēliyē payirai mēyntāl viḷaivatu eppaṭi’ eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyaik kūṟukiṉṟatu.
aṭi 31 - 32.  “elikkup payantu vīṭṭaic cuṭṭatu pōla” eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyaic cuṭṭukiṟatu. “eliye ciṟṟiccu illam cuṭṭāl eli cāṭiyum pōm, illam ventum pōm” eṉpatu malaiyāḷa nāṭṭup paḻamoḻi.
oru moḻi allātu irumoḻi āyiṉ - oruvar kaṭṭaḷai iṭāmal iruvar kaṭṭaḷaiyiṭṭāl eṉpatu karuttu.

kaṭipuri - kōṭṭai. ivuḷitēr - kutiraippaṭai, tērppaṭai. kaṭakayam - matam poruntiya yāṉai.  kaikkuṭkaṉi - uḷḷaṅkai nellik kaṉi.
kuṭilaṉaik kuṭilaṉ eṉṟē koṇṭēṉ - kuṭilaṉai vañcakaṉ eṉṟē karutiṉēṉ eṉpatu karuttu.  (kuṭilaṉ -vañcakaṉ). pakaṭi -kēli, parikācam.
poṟṟoṭi - poṉ vaḷaiyal.  ‘eṉ taṅkaiyiṉ iḻivu ippaṭi eṉakkē’-palatēvaṉuṭaiya cōrak kātaliyiṉ cakōtaraṉ palatēvaṉai vēlāl kuttiyapōtu coṉṉa col.
urattu - mārpilē. poṉṟiṉaṉ - iṟantāṉ. kūli - iṅkuk kaikkūli eṉpatu poruḷ. cuvāmit turōki - yajamāṉat turōki, aracat turōki, kōvil - araṇmaṉai. ōval il - nīṅkutal illāta.
kaṉaikaḻal - olikkiṉṟa vīrakkaḻal. veruvilēṉ - añca villai. piḻaittaṉar - piḻaiceytaṉar. māṭṭutir - māṭṭuṅkaḷ, kaḻuvēṟṟuṅkaḷ.
kaḻu patiṉāyiram vēṇṭum - nārāyaṇaṉaik kaḻuvilēṟṟiṉāl avaṉuṭaṉ kaḻuvēṟupavar patiṉāyirampēr uḷḷaṉar eṉpatu karuttu. caṭaiyaṉ - ivaṉ kuṭilaṉuṭaiya cēvakaṉ. kokkok - caṭaiyaṉ vikkaliṉāl uṇṭākum ōcai.
uṟṟaṉ - irukkiṟāṉ.
pāttiram - takuti. ‘keṭṭārkku ulakil naṭṭār illai’ eṉpatu, “keṭṭārkku naṭṭār kiḷaiyilum illai” eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyaik kuṟikkiṟatu.
cemittu - kṣamittu, poṟuttu.

tuvātacāntam - paṉṉiraṇṭiṉ muṭivu. puruvattukkumēlē paṉṉiraṇṭu viraṟ kiṭaiyuḷḷa iṭam. iṅkut tuvātacāntam eṉpataṟku maturai mānakaram eṉpatu poruḷ. pūlōka vaṭivamāka irukkiṟa virāṭ puruṣaṉukkut tuvātacānta iṭamāka irukkiṟa iṭam maturai eṉṟu kūṟuvar. nivātamā - uṟaiviṭamāka eṉṟum, (puyaṟ) kāṟṟu illāta iṭamāka eṉṟum iru poruḷ koḷḷalām. nayappiṟku - aṉpiṟku

kālak karappu - kālattiṉāl ēṟpaṭum maṟaippu. poṉṟiṉum - aḻintālum. poṟitta - niṟuviya. aṟṟa - talaiyaṟṟa. kavantam - talaiyillāta uṭampu;  pōrkkaḷattil talaiveṭṭappaṭṭa uṭampu. talaiveṭṭappaṭṭa pōrvīrariṉ uṭampu (kavantam) cila nēram pōrceyvatum uṇṭu. peṟṟi pōl - taṉmaipōla. uṉi - uṉṉi, niṉaittu. toṭariyal eṉa - caṅkilit toṭarpōla.


maruṅku - iṭam. maṉakkōḷ - maṉattiṟ koṇṭatu tātāṉmiyam - oṉṟu maṟṟoṉṟātal. ceyaṟkai vācaṉai eṉpatu poruḷ. kuṭilaṉōṭu paḻakiyataṉāl ēṟpaṭṭa kuṇam eṉpatu poruḷ. vemkarā - koṭiya mutalai.
aṭi 108.  mutalaippiṭi eṉpatu karuttu. “mūrkkaṉum mutalaiyum koṇṭatu viṭā” eṉpatu paḻamoḻi.
109 - 111 aṭikaḷ, naṇṭu kuñcakaḷai īṉṟavuṭaṉ cettut taṉ uṭampaiyē kuñcukaḷukku uṇavāka aḷikkiṟatu eṉpatu karuttu. pirānti - mayakkam. orumalar - oru pū; eṉṟatu maṉōṉmaṇiyai.
aṭi 124 - 126. “neṭumpuṉaluḷ vellum mutalai yaṭum puṉaliṉ, nīṅkiṉ ataṉaip piṟa” eṉṉum tirukkuṟaḷiṉ karuttai viḷakku kiṟatu. ayarntu - maṟantu. cālipuram - nelvēli, tirunelvēli. cālipuram eṉpataṟkut tirunelvēli eṉṟum, uṭampu eṉṟum iruporuḷ koḷka. pulap paṭai - pakaippulattup paṭai eṉṟum, aimpulappaṭai eṉṟum iru poruḷ koḷka. māpati - maturai nakaram eṉṟum, param poruḷākiya kaṭavuḷ eṉṟum iruporuḷ koḷka.
aḷittaruḷutiyēl - kāttaruḷiṉāl. cantatam - eppoḻutum. maṟali - yamaṉ. ciṟukoṭi - ciṟiya pūṅkoṭi pōṉṟa maṉōṉmaṇi.

174 - 175 aṭikaḷ, ‘There is many a slip between the cup and the lip’ eṉṉum āṅkilap paḻamoḻiyaik kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. piratipantaṅkaḷ - taṭaikaḷ. kukunāḷ - amāvācai nāḷ, uvā nāḷ. paravai - kaṭal. evaṉ - evvitam. camaḻppu - kuṟṟam, nāṇam. naḷi - tēḷ.
piraiyuṟu pāl eṉa - urai kuṟṟiya pālpōla. toṭu kaṟpaṭai - tōṇṭi amaikkappaṭṭa curaṅka vaḻi. kaḷavaḻi - kaḷḷa vaḻi, curaṅka vaḻi. nalital - varuntutal. kaḷaṅkam - vañcaṉai, mācu.
oṟuttu - varutti. cevvē vaṭakku - nēr vaṭakku. maṟu muṟi - aṭutta aṟai. (muṟi - aṟai. itu malaiyāḷa nāṭṭu vaḻakkuc  col.) 
235 - 243. aracaṉ pēccaik koṇṭē curaṅkam irukkum iṭattaik kuṭilaṉ yūkittaṟintukoṇṭu, ataittāṉ muṉṉamē aṟintavaṉ pōlap pēcuvatai ivvaṭikaḷ uṇarttukiṉṟaṉa.

pāṅkō - takutiyō. kuṇap piratam - naṉmai taruvatu tēṉār teriyal cūṭumuṉ - vaṇṭukaḷ moykkum mālaiyaṇivataṟku muṉpu; maṇam āvataṟkumuṉpu eṉpatu poruḷ. aniyar - anniyar. vatuvai muṉ - tirumaṇattukku muṉpu.

284 - 285 aṭikaḷiṉ karuttu, iravum pakalum cērntirātatupōla, kavalaiyum vīramum cērntu nillā eṉpatu. nuval taru - collappaṭum.
kallaṟai - curaṅkavaḻi. itupōl māṟṟalaṉ - puruṣōttamaṉaip pōla pakaivaṉ. pāṇṭamēl māṟṟalām - maṇkalattai viruppam pōl māṟṟi vāṅkalām, tirumaṇam appaṭippaṭṭataṉṟu eṉpatu karuttu. ētam - kuṟṟam.

344 - 345 aṭi, “kuṇanāṭik kuṟṟamu nāṭi yavaṟṟuḷ, mikaināṭi mikka koḷal” eṉṉum tirukkuṟaḷ karuttuṭaiyatu. mīṭkutum - (maṉōṉmaṇiyait) tirumpavum aḻaikkalām. ‘tappiṉaṉ nāraṇaṉ. cāṟṟaṟ kiṭamilai’ - nārāyaṇaṉ ciṟaiyil iruppatāl, maṉōṉmaṇi - palatēvaṉ tirumaṇattait taṭuttu niṟutta avaṉāl muṭiyātu eṉpatu karuttu.
viṭuttu - vāyviṭṭu. keṭupayal - palatēvaṉaik kuṟikkiṟatu. koṭiyaṉ - nārāyaṇaṉaik karutiṟṟu.
taṉ paṭaiyai evarum vella muṭiyātu eṉṟu niṉaittirunta jīvaka aracaṉ, pōril tōṟṟa piṉṉum taṉ kōṭṭaiyai mukkiyamāṉateṉṟu karutiyirukkiṟa taṉmai, ācai mutaliya paṟṟu nīṅki vairākkiyam uṇṭāṉālum, ñāṉamillāmal vairākkiyattai maṭṭum aṭaintavar, tam nilaiyai uṇarātatu pōṉṟatu ākum eṉpatu karuttu.
	aritā - (velluvataṟku) arumaiyāṉatu. aṅkaṅkaḷ - (cēṉaiyiṉ) uṟuppukaḷ. puri - kōṭṭai. punti - putti . cārpu nāṉ eṉatu eṉṉum paṟṟu. peyarntu - pōy. virākam - vairākkiyam, paṟṟu oḻittal. āṇavam - akaṅkāram. āṇavam, karmam, māyai eṉṉum mummalaṅkaḷil oṉṟu. āṉmākkaḷiṉ aṟivai maṟaippatu itaṉ kuṇam.

inta āṟu kuṟaḷveṇ centuṟaic ceyyuṭkaḷum (6 - āvatu ceyyaḷiṉ kaṭaici aṭiyait tavira) ñāṉacamātiyil	terikiṟa piramāṉupūtikkum, puruṭōttamaṉ kaṉavil kaṇṭa maṉōṉmaṇi yiṉ kātal vaṭivattiṟkum poruntiyiruppatu kāṇka.
anukūlam uraittāṉ - cēraṉ pōr toṭuttatu kaṉavai vilakkum poruṭṭu ; nāṭu piṭippatu alla. itu kuṭilaṉukku aṉukūlam. iṟutta - taṅkiya. kauvai - tuṉpam. jaṭiti - viraivāka, intustāṉic col. jalti eṉṟum vaḻaṅkukiṟatu. tatkṣaṇam - ippoḻutē, uṭaṉē.

142 - 144 aṭikaḷ. irāmaṉ ilaṅkaiyai veṉṟu vipīṣaṇaṉukku muṭicūṭṭiyatupōla kuṭilaṉākiya eṉakku muṭicūṭṭuvaiyāyiṉ naṉṟāka aracāḷvēṉ eṉṟu kuṭilaṉ kūṟiyatu. mēvarum - aṭaitaṟkarumaiyāṉa. kaitavaṉ - pāṇṭiyaṉ. kaitiyāy - ciṟaiyāḷaṉāka. (kaiti - intustāṉic col). taḷai - vilaṅku.

kuḷikai - māttirai. 

pāṅkuuḷa - pakkattil uḷḷa, amaḷi - paṭukkai, tattu - vipattu.
43 - 61. varikaḷ. maṉōṉmaṇiyaip palatēvaṉukku maṇam ceyvikka eṇṇiyiruppatai aracaṉ maṉōṉmaṇikkuk kūṟiya pōtu, taṉakku atu viruppamillāmal iruntum, taṉatu tantaikku māṟupēcak kūṭāteṉṉum karuttuṭaṉ avaḷ cammatam terivittatai mutal tōḻi ivvarikaḷil kūṟukiṟāḷ.
vallai - viraivil. vācanīr - maṇamuḷḷa nīr. muṉcurattu aḻuntiya aṉṟu - muṉṉorunāḷ kaṉavu kaṇṭu curattiṉāl varuntiya aṉṟaiya tiṉam. āṭiyil - mukam pārkkum kaṇṇāṭiyil.
tirumukam - kaṭitam. itu - vāṇikkum naṭarājaṉukkum naṭakkap pōkiṟa tirumaṇam. tuyaril - palatēvaṉai maṉōṉmaṇi maṇakkappōkum tuṉpattil.

iccaiyātikaḷ - ācai mutaliyaṉa. maṉakkaḷaṅkam - maṉattil paṭikiṟa kaṟai. mācukaḷ - aḻukkukaḷ. maṇṇiya - kaḻuviya. cāṇai - cāṇaikkal, māṇikkam mutaliya kaṟkaḷaip paṭṭai piṭippataṟku upayōkappaṭuttuvatu. toḻumpu - aṭimai. pantam - kaṭṭu. centī aintu - pañcākkiṉi. nāṉkupuṟattilum neruppuk kuḻiyum mēlē cūriyaṉum erikkumpōtu naṭuvil iruntu tavam ceyvataṟku utavuvatu. pōṉakam - uṇavu.
aḷittal - kāttal. cūḷ - capatam. veḷḷil - viḷāmaram. iṅku viḷāmpaḻam eṉpatu karuttu. yāṉai nōy eṉpatu viḷām paḻattukku uṇṭākum nōy. innōy koṇṭa viḷāṅ kaṉikkuḷ cataippaṟṟu irātu. ōṭu maṭṭum irukkum. itaṉai “yāṉai yuṇṭa viḷāṅkaṉi” eṉṟu kūṟuvar. vētakam ceyta - vēṟu paṭuttiya. pōtakam - yāṉai. yūtapam - yāṉaik kūṭṭattukkut talaimai tāṅkum yāṉai. pōtakayūtapam - eṉpatu araca yāṉai pōṉṟavaṉ eṉpatu poruḷ. itu maṉōṉmaṇiyiṉ maṉattaik kavarnta kātalaṉaik kuṟikkiṟatu.
“veḷḷilā meḷḷa viḻuṅki yiṅṅaṉam, vētakam ceyta pōtaka yūtapam” eṉṉum aṭiyiṉ karuttu: yāṉaiyuṇṭa viḷāṅkaṉiyil ōṭu tavira uḷḷē catai illātiruppatu pōla, maṉōṉmaṇiyiṉ maṉattaik kavarntu avaḷai veṟṟuṭampiṉaḷākkiyatu avaḷ kaṉavil kaṇṭu kātalitta aracaṉākiya yāṉai eṉpatu.

vāñcāvacam - aṉpiṉ vaḻipaṭṭatu. kaṭapaṭa caṭam - (kaṭam - kuṭam. paṭam - tuṇi. caṭam - uṭampu) tarkkavātikaḷ upayōkappaṭuttum col; āravārap pēccu eṉpatu karuttu.
kōṇilā - kōṇal illāta, nērmai uṭaiya. ammaṇi - maṉōṉmaṇi.
kāṭṭal - pāvaṉai ceytal. avvuḻi - anta iṭam. poruvarum - oppillāta.

mantirālōcaṉai - irakaciya ālōcaṉai.
maṉattiṟam - maṉavalimai. eḷḷarum - ikaḻtal illāta, kuṟṟamaṟṟa, māṉum - okkum, āl - ālamaram. poṉṟiyō - uyir viṭṭō. canti - kālam, nilaimai. koṟṟavai - aracacapai.
vaṉtiṟal - mikunta vallamai. tiṇmai - palam, vali āḷviṉai - muyaṟci. vāram  - aṉpu. aṭaiyavum - muḻuvatum. palitam - palaṉ taruvatu.
ayirppuṟār - cantēkappaṭamāṭṭār. tāpatam - tavam ceyyumiṭam. āpatam - āpattu. puraiyaṟu -kuṟṟam illāta. kavviya - paṟṟiya. kauvai - tuṉpam. āññai - kaṭṭaḷai. teruḷuṟu - teḷivuḷḷa. cūḻcci - yōcaṉai.
tiṉamaṇi - cūriyaṉ. nammaṇi - maṉōṉmaṇi.
koccakak kalippā - 1.  tuyara aḷakkar - tuṉpamākiya kaṭal. piṟakiṭal - piṉ vāṅkutal. itil kajēntira mōkṣak katai kūṟappaṭukiṟatu (purāṇak katai viḷakkattil kāṇka).
2. aracaṉ - turiyōtaṉātiyar. oru terivai - tirepati. tuvarainakar - tuvārakai, kaṇṇapirāṉuṭaiya nakaram. citaivu. aḻivu. itil tiraupatiyiṉ tukilurital katai kūṟappaṭukiṟatu (purāṇakkatai viḷakkattil kāṇka).
3. maṟali - yamaṉ. veṟi kaḻumi - mayakkam niṟaintu. poṟi aḻiyum - aṟivu aḻiyum. itil mārkkaṇṭēyaṉ katai kūṟappaṭukiṟatu (purāṇakkatai viḷakkattil kāṇka). ik koccakak kalippā mūṉṟum, potuvāṉa kaṭavuḷ vāḻttākavum, maṉōṉmaṇi kaṉavukaṇṭu  ataṉāl koṇṭa kātal maṉōnilaikkup poruntiyatākavum iruppatu kāṇka.
vañciyāṉ vañciyāṉ - vañci nāṭṭāṉ vañcaṉai ceyya māṭṭāṉ. teruṭṭa - teḷivupaṭutta. vantuḻi - vantapōtu.
naṇṇavum - aṭaiyavum. vēṭṭaiyēl - virumpuvāyāṉāl. ñāṭpiṭai - pōrkkaḷattil. tārā - mālaiyāka. eṇpaṭu - karutappaṭum. kaṇpaṭu - tuyilkiṉṟa. iṟumpūtu - viyappu, āccariyam. veruvalai - añcātē.
viḷḷā - viṇṭupōkāta, viṭṭup piriyāta. aruttam - poruḷ.
kaiyaṟṟa - ceyalaṟṟa. kuṟi - aṭaiyāḷam. avattai - nilaimai. ceṟivāy - nerukkamāy. tirukku - kuṟṟam. ikkalittuṟai, taṉ cutantaram viṭṭu aruḷvaḻi niṉṟa pōtam atītappaṭum muṟaiyaik kūṟukiṉṟatu.
*	In the Market place of the Athenians is an altar of Pity which divinity, as she is, above alt others, beneficent to human life and to the
	mutability of human affairs, is alone of all the Greeks reverenced by the Athenians.’-’Pausanias; Attics, c. xvil.
